**parameters**　[{"parameter": "in_scene", "label_zh": "輸入場景", "label_en": "Input Scene", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Map", "description_en": "One or more local or global scenes that will be packaged into a single .mspk file. Active scenes and .mapx files can be added as input."}, {"parameter": "output_file", "label_zh": "輸出文件", "label_en": "Output File", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "File", "description_en": "The output mobile scene package .mspk file."}, {"parameter": "in_locator", "label_zh": "輸入定位器 （可選）", "label_en": "Input Locator (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Address Locator", "description_en": "One or more locators (.loc file) that will be included in the mobile scene package. Note: Locators have the following restrictions: The locator cannot have an unknown coordinate system. The locator or any participating locator in a composite locator cannot be a geocoding service, including services from ArcGIS Enterprise or ArcGIS Online."}, {"parameter": "area_of_interest", "label_zh": "感興趣區域 （可選）", "label_en": "Area of Interest (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "A polygon layer that defines the area of interest. Only those features that intersect the area of interest will be included in the mobile scene package."}, {"parameter": "extent", "label_zh": "范圍 （可選）", "label_en": "Extent (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Extent", "description_en": "Specifies the extent that will be used to select or clip features. Current Display Extent —The extent will be based on the active map or scene. Draw Extent —The extent will be based on a rectangle drawn on the map or scene. Extent of a Layer —The extent will be based on an active map layer. Choose an available layer or use the Extent of data in all layers option. Each map layer has the following options: All Features —The extent of all features. Selected Features —The extent of the selected features. Visible Features —The extent of visible features. Browse —The extent will be based on a dataset. Intersection of Inputs —The extent will be the intersecting extent of all inputs. Union of Inputs —The extent will be the combined extent of all inputs. Clipboard —The extent can be copied to and from the clipboard. Copy Extent —Copies the extent and coordinate system to the clipboard. Paste Extent —Pastes the extent and coordinate system from the clipboard. If the clipboard does not include a coordinate system, the extent will use the map's coordinate system. Reset Extent —The extent will be reset to the default value. When coordinates are manually provided, the coordinates must be numeric values and in the active map's coordinate system. The map may use different display units than the provided coordinates. Use a negative value sign for south and west coordinates."}, {"parameter": "clip_features", "label_zh": "裁剪圖徵 （可選）", "label_en": "Clip Features (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether the output features will be clipped to the given area of interest or extent. Note: Multipatch feature layers, 3D point feature layers, LAS dataset layers, service layers, and tile packages cannot be clipped and will be completely copied to the mobile scene package. Checked—The geometry of the features will be clipped to the given area of interest or extent. Unchecked—Features in the map will be selected and their geometry will remain unaltered. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "title", "label_zh": "標題 （可選）", "label_en": "Title (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Title information that will be added to the properties of the package."}, {"parameter": "summary", "label_zh": "摘要 （可選）", "label_en": "Summary (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Summary information that will be added to the properties of the package."}, {"parameter": "description", "label_zh": "描述 （可選）", "label_en": "Description (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Description information that will be added to the properties of the package."}, {"parameter": "tags", "label_zh": "標簽 （可選）", "label_en": "Tags (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Tag information that will be added to the properties of the package. Multiple tags can be added, separated by a comma or semicolon."}, {"parameter": "credits", "label_zh": "配額 （可選）", "label_en": "Credits (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Credit information that will be added to the properties of the package."}, {"parameter": "use_limitations", "label_zh": "使用限制 （可選）", "label_en": "Use Limitations (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Use limitations that will be added to the properties of the package."}, {"parameter": "anonymous_use", "label_zh": "啟用匿名使用 （可選）", "label_en": "Enable Anonymous Use (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether the mobile scenes can be used by anyone or only those with an ArcGIS account. License: This optional parameter is only available with the Publisher extension. Checked—Anyone with access to the package can use the mobile scene without signing in with an Esri named user account. Unchecked—Anyone with access to the package must be signed in with a named user account to use the mobile scene. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "texture_optimization", "label_zh": "紋理優化 （可選）", "label_en": "Texture Optimization (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the textures that will be optimized according to the target platform where the scene layer package is used. Caution: Optimizations that include KTX2 may take significant time to process. For fastest results, use the Desktop or None options. All—All texture formats will be optimized including JPEG, DXT, and KTX2 for use in desktop, web, and mobile platforms. Desktop—Windows, Linux, and Mac supported textures will be optimized including JPEG and DXT for use in ArcGIS Pro clients on Windows and ArcGIS Maps SDKs desktop clients on Windows, Linux, and Mac. This is the default. Mobile—Android and iOS supported textures will be optimized including JPEG and KTX2 for use in ArcGIS Maps SDKs mobile applications. None—JPEG textures will be optimized for use in desktop and web platforms."}, {"parameter": "enable_scene_expiration", "label_zh": "啟用場景到期 （可選）", "label_en": "Enable Scene Expiration (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether the mobile scene package will time out. License: This optional parameter is only available with the Publisher extension. Checked—Time-out functionality will be enabled on the mobile scene package. Unchecked—Time-out functionality will not be enabled on the mobile scene package. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "scene_expiration_type", "label_zh": "場景到期類型 （可選）", "label_en": "Scene Expiration Type (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the type of scene access that will be used for the expired mobile scene package. License: This optional parameter is only available with the Publisher extension. Allow to open—The user of the package will be warned that the scene has expired and allowed to open the scene. This is the default. Do not allow to open—The user of the package will be warned that the scene has expired and will not be allowed to open the package."}, {"parameter": "expiration_date", "label_zh": "到期日期 （可選）", "label_en": "Expiration Date (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Date", "description_en": "The date the mobile scene package will expire. License: This optional parameter is only available with the Publisher extension."}, {"parameter": "expiration_message", "label_zh": "到期消息 （可選）", "label_en": "Expiration Message (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The text message that will appear when an expired scene is accessed. License: This optional parameter is only available with the Publisher extension."}, {"parameter": "select_related_rows", "label_zh": "僅保留與范圍內圖徵相關的行 （可選）", "label_en": "Keep only the rows that are related to features within the extent (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether the specified extent will be applied to related data sources. Checked—Only related data corresponding to records within the specified extent will be consolidated. Unchecked—Related data sources will be consolidated in their entirety. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "reference_online_content", "label_zh": "引用在線內容 （可選）", "label_en": "Reference online content (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether service layers will be referenced in the mobile package. Checked—Service layers will be referenced in the mobile package. Unchecked—Service layers will not be referenced in the mobile package. This is the default."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.management.CreateMobileScenePackage(in_scene, output_file, {in_locator}, {area_of_interest}, {extent}, {clip_features}, {title}, {summary}, {description}, {tags}, {credits}, {use_limitations}, {anonymous_use}, {texture_optimization}, {enable_scene_expiration}, {scene_expiration_type}, {expiration_date}, {expiration_message}, {select_related_rows}, {reference_online_content})

## TanH (Spatial Analyst 工具) · 用法

**id**　3d267b291ed9b2cd40-2

**english**　TanH

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Spatial Analyst 工具箱
- 數學分析工具集
- 三角函數

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/tanh.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/tanh.html

**text**　在數學中，所有三角函數都具有一個確定的有效輸入值范圍，該范圍稱為域。 每個函數中的輸出值也具有一個確定的范圍。 對于此工具，以下表述均為真： 域為：-∞ &lt; [in_value] &lt; ∞ 范圍為：-1 &lt; [out_value] &lt; 1 請注意，-∞ 和 ∞ 分別表示受特定網格格式支持的最小負值和最大正值。
該工具中的輸入和輸出值被看作是無單位量。
無論輸入資料為何種類型，輸出值都始終為浮點型。
如果輸入是多波段網格，則輸出也將為多波段網格。 該工具將在輸入中的每個波段上執行操作。
如果輸入是多維網格資料，則將處理所有變量的所有切片。 輸出將是 CRF 格式的多維網格。
有關適用于此工具的地理處理環境的詳細信息，請參閱分析環境和 Spatial Analyst。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/tanh.html#usage

**text**　# Name: TanH_Ex_02.py
# Description: Calculates the hyperbolic tangent of cells in a raster
# Requirements: Spatial Analyst extension

# Import system modules
import arcpy
from arcpy import env
from arcpy.sa import *

# Set environment settings
env.workspace = "C:/sapyexamples/data"

# Set local variables
inRaster = "degs"

# Run TanH
outTanH = TanH(inRaster)

# Save the output
outTanH.save("C:/sapyexamples/output/outtanh.img")

**text**　自動提交, 像元大小, 像元大小投影方法, 當前工作空間, 范圍, 地理變換, 掩膜, 輸出配置關鍵字, 輸出座標系, 臨時工作空間, 捕捉網格, 分塊大小

## TanH (Spatial Analyst 工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Spatial Analyst 工具箱
- 數學分析工具集
- 三角函數

**english**　TanH

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/tanh.html

**id**　3d267b291ed9b2cd40-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/tanh.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 TanH，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入網格資料或常量值 [in_raster_or_constant]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/tanh.html#parameters

## 001987: 公共設施網絡需要域，無法修改。 · 描述

**id**　3d29208f4e5f70a5b9-1

**english**　001987: The domain is required by utility network and cannot be modified.

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告
- 1901-2000

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-01976-02000-001987.html#description

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-01976-02000-001987.html

**text**　最初啟用網絡拓撲之后，將無法修改分配給資產類型欄位的屬性域。 其中包括嘗試修改資產類型欄位的屬性域分配，或者更改分配給資產類型欄位的現有屬性域代碼或描述值。
有關其他示例和所需的網絡拓撲狀態，請參閱 公共設施網絡管理任務 。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-01976-02000-001987.html#description

## 訓練 ISO 聚類分類器 (影像分析工具) · 用法

**id**　3d3bf2655701f3f2ae-2

**english**　Train ISO Cluster Classifier

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 影像分析工具箱
- 分類和模式識別工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/image-analyst/train-iso-cluster-classifier.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/image-analyst/train-iso-cluster-classifier.html

**text**　任何 Esri 支持的網格都可用作輸入，包括網格產品、分割網格、鑲嵌、影像服務或通用網格資料集。 分割網格必須為 8 位 3 波段網格。
分割影像屬性參數僅在其中一個網格圖層輸入為分割影像時處于活動狀態。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/image-analyst/train-iso-cluster-classifier.html#usage
